\displaystyle=\frac{{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}}{{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}}=\frac{{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}

\displaystyle{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}

\displaystyle={\bm{A}}^{+}-\frac{{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}-\frac{{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}+\frac{{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}}{({\underline{\gamma}}_{A2})^{2}}

Before presenting the derivations for
E1
-
E6
, it is important to note that further expanding
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}
is already complex due to its nested nature. For example, we need to calculate the quadratic and cubic powers of
{\bm{A}}^{+}
, and when considering
E4
-
E6
, we will even have to consider
\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{4}
. Simplifying these calculations first would be beneficial for subsequent proofs. Therefore, the following
C.3.2.1
and
C.3.2.2
will present necessary results, especially for
{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}
,
{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}{\bm{b}}
,
{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{3}{\bm{b}}
, and
{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{4}{\bm{b}}
.
C.3.2.1
provides some intermediate results, while
C.3.2.2
contains the proofs for the results in
C.3.2.1
.

C.3.2.1

{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}

(Please note that we use underlining to denote terms that appear in the
\rho>1
case.)

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{14}

\displaystyle=\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}-\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{15}

\displaystyle={\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}=0

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{16}

\displaystyle={\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{1}{(\rho-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{17}

\displaystyle={\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}-\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{1}{(\rho-1)^{2}}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{18}

\displaystyle=\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{19}

\displaystyle=\bm{h}_{{\bm{B}}}\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{20}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}\frac{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)^{2}\rho}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}(\rho-1)}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{21}

\displaystyle={\bm{u}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}-\sigma_{f}^{2}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{22}

\displaystyle=\bm{h}_{{\bm{A}}}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}\sigma_{f}^{2}\frac{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)(\rho-\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}})}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}(\rho-1)}

\displaystyle{\underline{\xi}}_{23}

\displaystyle={\bm{u}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}\bm{k}_{{\bm{A}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{(\rho-1)^{2}}

Here we present some useful results that are repeatedly used. We use
A\Rightarrow B
to denote that considering
A
is equivalent to considering
B
asymptotically.

1.

{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}{\bm{B}}^{+}\Rightarrow{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}

2.

{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}^{+}\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}{\underline{\xi}}_{7}\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\xi}}_{7}{\underline{\xi}}_{14}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\xi}}_{9}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}}{\underline{\xi}}_{9}{\underline{\xi}}_{16}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}

3.

\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}^{+}\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{2}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}}(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{2}}{\bm{q}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}

4.

{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)\Rightarrow{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)+{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}

5.

{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\underline{\zeta}}_{4}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{11}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{16}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}

6.

{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}\Rightarrow{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\bm{u}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}\bm{h}_{{\bm{A}}}

7.

{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}\Rightarrow-\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}\bm{h}_{{\bm{A}}}+{\tilde{\beta}}_{{\bm{A}}}\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}{\bm{u}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}

8.

{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}\Rightarrow{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}-{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}-{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}{\underline{\xi}}_{22}+{\tilde{\beta}}_{{\bm{A}}}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}

9.

{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}=\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}-\frac{1}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}\left(\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}+\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{3}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}+{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{3}\right)\\
+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}\left(\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}+{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{2}\right)+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A4}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}-\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}^{2}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{3}}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}

C.3.2.2
Proofs for
C.3.2.1

We omit tedious and lengthy proofs for most of the terms but retain the most important and fundamental ones. Specifically, we omit the proofs for the
{\underline{\zeta}}
and
{\underline{\xi}}
terms, as they are transformations of other terms. Additionally, we omit most of the
{\underline{\alpha}}
,
{\underline{\beta}}
and
{\underline{\gamma}}
terms involving
{\bm{B}}
,
{\bm{A}}
, and
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
, since the
{\bm{B}}
-related terms are entirely based on terms in
{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}
, the
{\bm{A}}
-related terms are based on
{\bm{B}}
, and the
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
-related terms are based on
{\bm{A}}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{0}
: Consider the square of
{\underline{\beta}}_{0}
, we have

T{\underline{\beta}}_{0}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle-{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\beta}}_{0}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}
: Consider the square of
{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}
, we have

T{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle-{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\beta}}_{S11}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{IS}
: Based on the above two results, we have
{\underline{\beta}}_{IS}=O_{p}\left(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}
: Consider the square of
{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}
, we have

T{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}^{2}

\displaystyle-{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{S2}
: Similar to the proof for
{\underline{\beta}}_{S1}
, we have
{\underline{\beta}}_{S2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\underline{\beta}}_{B11}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\beta}}_{B11}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)+{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}

\displaystyle={\underline{\beta}}_{S11}+{\underline{\xi}}_{7}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{9}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{0}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{0}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{0}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{0}

\displaystyle-\rho=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\rho
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}

\displaystyle-1=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}1
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S11}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S11}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S11}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}

\displaystyle-\rho=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\rho
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{2}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\frac{T}{N}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{1}{p}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right)^{+}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\rho}\frac{1}{1-\frac{1}{\rho}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S3}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S3}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S3}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{2}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Based on the same justification in
D3
,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S3}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S4}
: Based on trace Lemma
1
we have

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{4}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{S4}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\Tr\left[\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\right]=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Based on the same justification in
D3
,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{S4}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\rho(\rho+1)}{(\rho-1)^{5}}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\alpha}}_{IB}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\alpha}}_{IB}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{I}_{N}-{\bm{B}}{\bm{B}}^{+}\right)\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle={\underline{\alpha}}_{IS}-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\underline{\alpha}}_{S1}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\underline{\xi}}_{1}{\underline{\xi}}_{4}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}-{\underline{\xi}}_{1}{\underline{\xi}}_{5}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{3}{\underline{\xi}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{3}{\underline{\xi}}_{8}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}.

Thus,
{\underline{\alpha}}_{IB}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\gamma}}_{IS}
:

\displaystyle\gamma_{IS}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}\left(\bm{I}_{N}-{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\right){\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[\left({\bm{\mathcal{S}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}\left({\bm{\mathcal{S}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)-\left({\bm{\mathcal{S}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\right]{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0^{+}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left[\lambda\left({\bm{\mathcal{S}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}\right]{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\lambda\rightarrow 0^{+}}\lambda\left\|{\bm{b}}\right\|^{2}m(-\lambda)

\displaystyle=\left\|{\bm{b}}\right\|^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}.

Thus,
{\underline{\gamma}}_{IS}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\gamma}}_{IB}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\gamma}}_{IB}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}\left(\bm{I}_{N}-{\bm{B}}{\bm{B}}^{+}\right){\bm{b}}

\displaystyle={\underline{\gamma}}_{IS}-{\underline{\xi}}_{4}{\underline{\xi}}_{1}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{6}{\underline{\xi}}_{3}-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}{\underline{\gamma}}_{S1}-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}{\underline{\gamma}}_{IS}{\underline{\xi}}_{1}+\frac{\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}{\underline{\xi}}_{9}{\underline{\xi}}_{3}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow{\underline{\gamma}}_{IS}.

Thus,
{\underline{\gamma}}_{IB}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tilde{b}^{2}\frac{\rho-1}{\rho}
.

Limit of
{\underline{\gamma}}_{B1}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\gamma}}_{B1}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\gamma}}_{S1}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\xi}}_{7}{\underline{\xi}}_{1}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\xi}}_{9}{\underline{\xi}}_{3}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{\tilde{b}^{2}}{\rho(\rho-1)}.

Thus,
{\underline{\gamma}}_{B1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{\tilde{b}^{2}}{\rho(\rho-1)}
.

Now we are able to derive asymptotics for
E1
-
E6
. Similar to case of
\rho<1
, we organize the following into three parts: First, in
C.3.2.2
, we present the asymptotic results
for
E1
-
E6
. Second, in
C.3.2.4
, we list the asymptotic results for the intermediate terms that appear in
C.3.2.3
. Third, in
C.3.2.5
, we provide the proofs for the asymptotic results of the intermediate terms.

Recall that
\tilde{\phi}=\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}\tilde{b}^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}
, thus
\frac{\mu_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}=\frac{\tilde{\phi}+1}{\tilde{\phi}}{\tilde{\theta}}
,
\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}=\frac{\tilde{\phi}}{\rho}+1
.

C.3.2.3
Asymptotic results of
E1
-
E6

Limit of
E1
: We have

\displaystyle E1

\displaystyle={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{\mu}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}^{2}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}^{2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\mu_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}\frac{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)(\rho\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-\rho+1)}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}(\rho-1)}=\frac{(\tilde{\phi}+1)^{2}\rho{\tilde{\theta}}}{(\tilde{\phi}+\rho)^{2}(\rho-1)}.

Limit of
E2
: We have

\displaystyle E2

\displaystyle={\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=E21+E22+2E23,

where

\displaystyle E21

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}^{2}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=\sigma_{f}^{2}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle E22

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\rho-1},

\displaystyle E23

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right){\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-1}).

Thus,
E2\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}
.

Limit of
E3
: We have

\displaystyle E3

\displaystyle={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=E31+E32,

where

\displaystyle E31

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mu_{f}\sigma_{f}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right){\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\mu_{f}\sigma_{f}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right){\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle E32

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
E3=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
E4
: We have

\displaystyle E4

\displaystyle={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{\mu}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}^{2}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\sigma_{f}^{2}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{I}_{N}\right){\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}^{2}\sigma_{f}^{2}\left({\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}\right)^{2}+\mu_{f}^{2}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\left({\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}^{2}\sigma_{f}^{2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}^{2}+\mu_{f}^{2}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\mu_{f}^{2}}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}\frac{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{4}}\frac{1}{(\rho-1)^{2}}(\rho\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-\rho+1)\left[(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)(\rho-1)+\frac{\rho\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}}{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)(\rho-1)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho{\tilde{\theta}}\tilde{\phi}^{2}(\tilde{\phi}+1)^{2}}{(\rho-1)(\tilde{\phi}+\rho)^{4}}+\frac{\rho^{2}{\tilde{\theta}}(\tilde{\phi}+1)^{2}}{(\rho-1)^{3}(\tilde{\phi}+\rho)^{2}}.

Limit of
E5
: We have

\displaystyle E5

\displaystyle={\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\sigma_{f}^{2}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{I}_{N}\right){\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=E51+E52+2E53+E54+E55+2E56,

where

\displaystyle E51

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}^{4}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}\left({\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}\right)^{2}=\sigma_{f}^{4}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}^{2}=O_{p}(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle E52

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}^{2}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}^{2}=O_{p}(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle E53

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}^{3}\sigma_{\epsilon}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle E54

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}^{2}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}=O_{p}(T^{-1})

\displaystyle E55

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}},

\displaystyle E56

\displaystyle=\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}=O_{p}(T^{-1})

(C.7)

Thus,
E5\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}
.

Limit of
E6
: We have

\displaystyle E6

\displaystyle={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\sigma_{f}^{2}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}+\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\bm{I}_{N}\right){\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}{\bm{b}}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=E61+E62+E63+E64,

where

\displaystyle E61

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}\sigma_{f}^{3}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\left({\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}=\mu_{f}\sigma_{f}^{3}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right){\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle E62

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}\sigma_{f}^{2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle E63

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}\sigma_{f}{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}\left(\frac{{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle E64

\displaystyle=\mu_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
E6=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

C.3.2.5
Proofs for
C.3.2.4

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}
, we have

{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}^{2}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right).

Based on trace Lemma
1
and same justification in
D3
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S1}
: By the same justification in
D3
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S1}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S1}
: By the same justification in
D2
, we have

{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S1}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}
, we have

T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\frac{1}{T}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\bm{e}^{\top}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\bm{e}^{\top}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\frac{1}{T}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}^{2}

\displaystyle-{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S11}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S11}
, we have

{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S11}^{2}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right).

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Based on
{\underline{\gamma}}_{S1}
, we have
{\bm{b}}^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{b}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tilde{b}^{2}/\rho
. Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S11}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S11}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S11}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right)^{-2}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\right)^{-1}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right).

Thus,
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S11}-\rho\frac{1}{p}\Tr(I_{T})=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
,
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S11}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}1
.

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{1}
:

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}
, we have

T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\frac{1}{T}\bm{e}^{\top}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\rho^{3}}\frac{1+1/\rho}{(1-1/\rho)^{5}}=\frac{\rho^{2}+\rho}{(\rho-1)^{5}}.

Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{IB}
:
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{IB}={\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{0}-{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B11}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{8}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{8}=\frac{1}{\left\|{\bm{u}}_{{\bm{A}}}\right\|^{2}}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{IB}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}=\frac{\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\left\|{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}\right\|^{2}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}-{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B11}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}
, we have

T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\frac{1}{T}\bm{e}^{\top}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{H}\bm{Y}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\rho.

Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B11}
: Based on useful result 4 in
C.3.2.1
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B11}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S11}+{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{1}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{3}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{1}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{4}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{3}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{6}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{10}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{10}=\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{11}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{11}={\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B1}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{B1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{B1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S1}+\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{1}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{4}-\frac{1}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{3}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{6}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle={\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{B1}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{11}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{8}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{10}+\beta_{{\bm{A}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{8}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle={\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B1}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{11}{\underline{\xi}}_{10}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}{\underline{\xi}}_{11}+\beta_{{\bm{A}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{9}{\underline{\xi}}_{10}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A2}
: Based on useful result 7 in
C.3.2.1
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{10}+{\tilde{\beta}}_{{\bm{A}}}\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{8}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A2}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{+}{\bm{A}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{B2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S2}
: By the same justification in
D5
, we have

{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{S2}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}
: Consider the square of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}
, we have

T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}^{2}=\frac{1}{T}{\bm{z}}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\frac{1}{T}\bm{e}^{\top}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}.

Based on trace Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{T}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}\bm{H}\bm{Y}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{3}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\right]^{5}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle T{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1).

Thus,
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{12}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{12}=-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{13}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{13}=\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{14}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{14}=-\frac{\left\|{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}\right\|^{2}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{13}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{15}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{15}
:
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{15}=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S2}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S2}
: By the same justification in
D6
, we have

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S2}=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B2}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{S2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B2}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{B}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{16}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{16}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}

\displaystyle=-\left\|\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}-\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\bm{k}_{{\bm{B}}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S3}=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}})

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B31}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B31}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{B}}^{+}\right]^{3}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left[{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)+{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{B}}}^{\top}{\underline{\zeta}}_{5}-\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\bm{p}}_{{\bm{B}}}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\left[{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}+\frac{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{16}{\underline{\zeta}}_{6}\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B21}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B21}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{B}}^{+}\right]^{2}{\bm{B}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{4}}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}^{+}\left[\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}{\bm{\mathcal{S}}}+\left(\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right){\bm{b}}^{\top}\right]\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{H}{\bm{z}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S1}+\frac{\sigma_{f}}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{3}}{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{S2}{\underline{\beta}}_{0}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{17}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{17}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}}{\left\|{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}\right\|^{2}}\left[{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B1}-{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B21}\right]

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A3}
: Based on results 8 and 11:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A3}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}\left[{\bm{A}}^{+}\right]^{3}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\bm{B}}^{+}\left[{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}-{\bm{B}}^{+}{\bm{b}}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}-{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}{\underline{\xi}}_{22}+{\tilde{\beta}}_{{\bm{A}}}{\bm{v}}_{{\bm{A}}}^{+}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}\sigma_{f}\sigma_{\epsilon}\left({\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B2}-{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}_{B31}\right)-{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{B2}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}-{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{17}{\underline{\xi}}_{22}+{\tilde{\beta}}_{{\bm{A}}}{\underline{\tilde{\zeta}}}_{17}{\underline{\xi}}_{21}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle={\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}-\frac{{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}-\frac{{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle={\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A1}-\frac{{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A2}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}-\frac{{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A2}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{A1}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow{\tilde{\tilde{\underline{\alpha}}}}_{A2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{\epsilon}^{2}}\frac{\rho}{(\rho-1)^{3}}.

Limit of
{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}
:

\displaystyle{\tilde{\underline{\beta}}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{T}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}\right)^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(T^{-\frac{1}{2}}).

Limit of
{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}1}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle={\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}-{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}-\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}^{2}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{f}^{2}}\frac{(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1)(\rho\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-\rho+1)}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}(\rho-1)}

Limit of
{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}
:

\displaystyle{\underline{\gamma}}_{{\widehat{\Sigma}}2}

\displaystyle={\bm{b}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{+}{\bm{b}}

\displaystyle={\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}-\frac{1}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}}\left({\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}+{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}+{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}\left({\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}+{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}\right)+\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A4}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{2}}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}^{2}-\frac{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A3}^{2}}{{\underline{\gamma}}_{A2}^{3}}{\underline{\gamma}}_{A1}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{f}^{4}}\frac{1}{v_{{\bm{A}}}^{2}}\left(\frac{\sigma_{f}^{2}}{\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\rho-1}\right)^{2}\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-1\right)^{2}\left(\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}+\rho-1\right)\left(\rho\tilde{\sigma}_{{\bm{B}}}-\rho+1\right)

∎

Appendix D
Asymptotic Results for Ridge Regularized Estimator

In this section, our examination focus on the asymptotic performance of the ridge regularized estimator.

D.1
Ridge Regularized Estimator

Definition 4

(Regularized Estimator)
.

Given the sample mean vector
{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}
and the sample covariance matrix
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
(defined in main body Section
2
) from observed data, along with a specified risk constraint
\sigma
, we define the following estimator:

\displaystyle\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}=\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}},

(D.8)

where
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}=\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}
and
\lambda>0
the regularized parameter.

D.2
Assumptions

D1
High Dimensionality.

We consider asymptotic setup where
T,N\rightarrow\infty
, and
\rho_{T}\coloneq N/T\rightarrow\rho\in(0,1)\cup(1,\infty)
.
∎

D2
DGP.

The excess returns vector at time
t
is generated as
\bm{r}_{t}={\bm{\mu}}+{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{y}}_{t}
, where
{\bm{y}}_{t}
is independently and identically distributed following
\mathcal{N}\left(\bm{0},\bm{I}_{N}\right)
for each time period
t
.
∎

Note that normality assumption is common in portfolio optimization. It is more strict than Assumption
A2
, which assumes an elliptical distribution for
{\bm{y}}_{t}
to allow for more tail flexibility. The main reason for assuming normality is for technical proof: it ensures the independence of the sample mean and variance - a property unique to the normal distribution - therefore facilitating certain theoretical proofs.

D3
Population Covariance Matrix.

(a)

The population covariance matrix
{\bm{\Sigma}}
is a deterministic
N\times N
positive semidefinite matrix.

(b)

Denote by
{\bm{\Sigma}}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\tau_{i}\bm{\upsilon}_{i}\bm{\upsilon}_{i}^{\top}
the eigenvalue decomposition of
{\bm{\Sigma}}
with
\tau_{1}\geq\tau_{2}\geq\dots\geq\tau_{N}\geq 0
. The empirical spectral distribution (ESD) of population covariance matrix is defined as:
\widehat{H}_{N}(\tau)=N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{I}_{\tau_{i}\leq\tau}
. It is assumed that
\widehat{H}_{N}
converges to a limiting law
H
, which is referred to as the limiting spectral distribution (LSD).

(c)

The support of
H
,
Supp(H)
, is included in a compact interval
[M_{1},M_{2}]
, with
0<M_{1}\leq M_{2}<\infty
.

(d)

\limsup_{N\rightarrow\infty}\tau_{1}<M
,
\liminf_{N\rightarrow\infty}\tau_{N}>0
,
\int\tau^{-1}\,d\widehat{H}_{N}(\tau)<M
.

∎

The assumption of a limiting population spectral distribution is common in large-dimensional asymptotics. Specifically,
(b)
describes the cross-sectional distribution of population eigenvalues, denoted as
\widehat{H}_{N}
. The subscript
N
in
\widehat{H}_{N}
is to emphasize its dependence on a particular
{\bm{\Sigma}}
with specific dimensionality. Note that the condition
(c)
does not imply the fulfillment of
(d)
. This distinction arises because a minor fraction [expressed formally as
o(N)
] of the eigenvalues of
{\bm{\Sigma}}
may either escalate indefinitely or diminish to zero, even though
(c)
is satisfied. The last condition in
(c)
requires the eigenvalues of
{\bm{\Sigma}}
not to accumulate near 0.

D4
Reweighted Spectral Distribution.

The empirical distribution of the expected returns
{\bm{\mu}}
, projected on the basis of eigenvectors
\left(\langle{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\upsilon}_{1}\rangle,...,\langle{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\upsilon}_{N}\rangle\right)
is defined as:

{\widehat{G}}_{N}(\tau)=\dfrac{1}{\left\|{\bm{\mu}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\langle{\bm{\mu}},\bm{\upsilon}_{i}\rangle^{2}\mathbb{I}_{\tau_{i}\leq\tau}.

It is assumed that
{\widehat{G}}_{N}
converges to a limiting law
G
.
∎

The distribution
{\widehat{G}}_{N}
represents the empirical distribution, reweighted through the projection of
{\bm{\mu}}
onto the eigenvectors
\bm{\upsilon}_{i}
of the covariance matrix
{\bm{\Sigma}}
. This reweighting process emphasizes the influence of
{\bm{\mu}}
’s orientation relative to the principal components defined by
{\bm{\Sigma}}
’s eigenvectors. In finance, this distribution allows investors to gauge whether expected returns are aligned with or diverge from the principal risk directions of the market.

D5
Sparse Mean Vector.

Assume that the mean vector
{\bm{\mu}}
is sparse such that as
N\rightarrow\infty
,
\left\|{\bm{\mu}}(N)\right\|_{2}^{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tilde{\xi}^{2}
.
∎

D6
Maximum Theoretical Sharpe Ratio.

Assume that the square of theoretical Sharpe ratio,
{\theta}
(equivalently,
{{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}}
), is a “function” of
N
, represented as
{\theta(N)}
. As
N
increases,
{\theta(N)}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}{{\tilde{\theta}}}
, where
{{\tilde{\theta}}}
is bounded away from 0 and infinity.
∎

D.3
Main results

First, we introduce some notations used in the following Theorems. Let
m_{F_{{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}}}(z)
represent the Stieltjes transform of the empirical spectral distribution (ESD) of sample covariance matrix
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
. The ESD is defined as
F_{{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}}(d)=N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{I}_{d_{i}\leq d}
, where
d_{1}\geq\dots\geq d_{N}
are eigenvalues of
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
. Accordingly, the Stieltjes transform is given by:

m_{F_{{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}}}(z)=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{d_{i}-z}=\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}-z\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}\right].

Also, we denote by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}m(z)}
the limit that
m_{F_{{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}}}(z)
converges to almost surely. The limit
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}m(z)}
is the solution of the following equation (detailed in
Marchenko and Pastur, (1967)
):

m(z)=\int\dfrac{1}{\tau\left(1-\rho-\rho zm(z)\right)}dH(\tau).

(D.9)

Further define
m_{1}(z)
via

\displaystyle m_{1}(z):=\frac{\int\frac{\tau^{2}[1-\rho-\rho zm(z)]}{[\tau[1-\rho-\rho zm(z)]-z]^{2}}\,dH(\tau)}{1-\rho\int\frac{z\tau}{[\tau[1-\rho-\rho zm(z)]-z]^{2}}\,dH(\tau)}.

To facilitate a deeper understanding of our analysis, under Assumption (
D1
-
D5
), we introduce and define several key intermediate terms:

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-\lambda m(-\lambda)}{1-\rho\left(1-\lambda m(-\lambda)\right)},

\displaystyle\Theta_{2}(\lambda,\rho)

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1-\lambda m(-\lambda)}{\left[1-\rho+\rho\lambda m(-\lambda)\right]^{3}}-\lambda\dfrac{m(-\lambda)-\lambda m^{\prime}(-\lambda)}{\left[1-\rho+\rho\lambda m(-\lambda)\right]^{4}},

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}(\lambda,\rho,H,G)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\xi}^{2}\int\frac{1}{\lambda\left[1+\left(\rho m(-\lambda)+\frac{\rho-1}{\lambda}\right)\tau\right]}dG(\tau),

\displaystyle\Phi_{2}(\lambda,\rho,H,G)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\xi}^{2}\left(1+\rho m_{1}(-\lambda)\right)\int\frac{\tau}{[\lambda+(1-\rho+\rho\lambda m(-\lambda))\tau]^{2}}dG(\tau).

Theorem 5

(Sharpe ratio)
.

Under Assumptions (
D1
-
D5
), it holds in probability that

SR\left[\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}\right]\rightarrow\dfrac{\Phi_{1}}{\sqrt{\Phi_{2}+\rho\Theta_{2}}}.

Theorem 6

(Out-of-sample prediction loss)
.

Under Assumptions (
D1
-
D6
), it holds in probability that

L_{{\bm{R}}}\left[\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}};{{\bm{\omega}^{\ast}}}\right]\rightarrow\sigma^{2}\left[\dfrac{\Phi_{1}^{2}+\Phi_{2}+\rho\Theta_{2}}{\Phi_{1}+\rho\Theta_{1}}-2\dfrac{\Phi_{1}\left(1+{\tilde{\theta}}\right)}{\sqrt{\Phi_{1}+\rho\Theta_{1}}\sqrt{{\tilde{\theta}}}}+1+{\tilde{\theta}}\right].

D.4
Proofs for Theorem
5
,
6

Proof:
.

Let us first recall that the ridge regularized estimator is defined by

\displaystyle\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}=\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}},

then the prediction loss of this estimator can be expanded using some key intermediate terms

\displaystyle L_{{\bm{R}}}\left[\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}};{{\bm{\omega}^{\ast}}}\right]

\displaystyle={B_{{\bm{R}}}\left(\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}};{{\bm{\omega}^{\ast}}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}-{{\bm{\omega}^{\ast}}}\right)^{\top}\left({\bm{\Sigma}}+{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\right)\left(\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}-{{\bm{\omega}^{\ast}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}\left[\dfrac{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}-2\dfrac{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{{\bm{\mu}}}}{\sqrt{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}\sqrt{{{\bm{\mu}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{-1}{{\bm{\mu}}}}}+1\right.

\displaystyle\quad\text{ }+\left.\dfrac{\left({\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right)^{2}}{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}-2\dfrac{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\sqrt{{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{-1}{{\bm{\mu}}}}}{\sqrt{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}}+{{{\bm{\mu}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\Sigma}}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}}\right].

The Sharpe ratio can be expanded as

\displaystyle SR\left[\widehat{{\bm{\omega}}^{\ast}_{\lambda}}\right]=\dfrac{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}}{\sqrt{{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}}}.

We denote

F1={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}
,
F2={\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
,
F3={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
,
F4={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}
,
F5={\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
,
F6={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
, with
{\widehat{\bm{m}}}={\widehat{{\bm{\mu}}}}-{\bm{\mu}}
.

Limit of
F1
:
By using the anisotropic local law of Theorem 3.16 in
Knowles and Yin, (2017)
, we obatin that for any
D>0,\varepsilon>0
, where exists
C=C(D,\varepsilon)
such that, with probability at least
1-CT^{-D}
,

\left|{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}-\dfrac{1}{\lambda}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left[\bm{I}_{N}+r(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right|\leq\sqrt{\dfrac{{\operatorname{Im}}\left(r(-\lambda)\right)}{{\operatorname{Im}}\left(-\lambda\right)T^{1-\varepsilon}}}

uniformly over
{\operatorname{Re}}(\lambda)>T^{-2/3+(1/{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M})},{\operatorname{Im}}(-\lambda)>0
, where
{\operatorname{Re}}
and
{\operatorname{Im}}
are real and imaginar part of a complex number. Here
r(z)
is defined as the unique solution of

\dfrac{1}{r(z)}=-z+\rho\int\dfrac{\tau}{1+\tau r(z)}dH(\tau).

Existence and uniqueness of the solution
r(z)
is given, for instance, in Lemma 2.2 of
Knowles and Yin, (2017)
. This lemma also states that
r(z)
is the Stieltjes transorm of a probability measure with support in
[0,C({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}M})]
. As a consequence, for
\lambda=\lambda_{1}+i\lambda_{2},\lambda_{1}>0

\displaystyle{\operatorname{Im}}(r(-\lambda))

\displaystyle={\operatorname{Im}}\int\frac{1}{x+\lambda}d\pi(x)=\int\frac{-\lambda_{2}}{(x+\lambda_{1})^{2}+\lambda_{2}^{2}}d\pi(x)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\left|{\operatorname{Im}}(r(-\lambda))\right|\leq\dfrac{\left|{\operatorname{Im}}(\lambda)\right|}{{\operatorname{Re}}(\lambda)^{2}}.

Therefore,

\left|{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}-\dfrac{1}{\lambda}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left[\bm{I}_{N}+r(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right|\leq\dfrac{1}{\left|{\operatorname{Re}}(\lambda)\right|T^{\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}}}.

Also,

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\lambda}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left[\bm{I}_{N}+r(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{\lambda}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left[\bm{I}_{N}+\left(\rho m(-\lambda)+\frac{\rho-1}{\lambda}\right){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\|{\bm{\mu}}\|^{2}}\int\frac{1}{\lambda\left[1+\left(\rho m(-\lambda)+\frac{\rho-1}{\lambda}\right)\tau\right]}dG(\tau),

where the first equality follows by the definition

m(z)=\dfrac{1-\rho+zr(z)}{\rho z}.

Limit of
F2
:
Recall that
{\widehat{\bm{m}}}={\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T
, thus we have

\displaystyle{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}=

\displaystyle\left(\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right),

where
{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}=\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T
. Note that
\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}
is considered a vector of
N
random variables, each following a normal distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
. In accordance with Assumption
D2
(Normality Assumption), the sample mean and sample covariance matrix are independent. This independence allows us to invoke the trace Lemma (Lemma
1
), yielding:

\dfrac{T}{N}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\rightarrow 0,\quad\text{in probability}.

By applying the technique of using the Stieltjes transform of the empirical spectral distribution of a matrix proposed in
Ledoit and Péché, (2011)
, we have

\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho),

(D.10)

where
\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho)=\dfrac{1-\lambda m(-\lambda)}{1-\rho\left(1-\lambda m(-\lambda)\right)}
and
m(-\lambda)
is defined in (
D.9
). Therefore,

{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\rho\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho).

Here is the detailed proof of (
D.10
): Denote the resolvent of
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
by
{\mathcal{R}}(z)=\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}-z\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}
. Note that the sample covariance matrix
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\bm{y}}_{t}{\bm{y}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
, by denoting
{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}{\bm{y}}_{t}
, we can rewrite
{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
. Therefore, for any
1\leq t\leq T
,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{R}}(z)

\displaystyle=\left[\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)-z\bm{I}_{N}+{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]^{-1}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z)-\dfrac{{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z)}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}},

(D.11)

where
{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z)=\left[\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}-{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)-z\bm{I}_{N}\right]^{-1}
. Note that
{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}
and
{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(z)
are independent for all
z\in\mathbb{C}
.

Multiplying
{\mathcal{R}}(z)
to both sides of the identity
\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}-z\bm{I}_{N}\right)+z\bm{I}_{N}={\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}
, we have

\bm{I}_{N}+z{\mathcal{R}}(z)={\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}{\mathcal{R}}(z)=\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}(z).

(D.12)

Now let us set
z=-\lambda
. Taking trace on both sides of (
D.12
), dividing both sides by
N
, and using (
D.11
), we have

\displaystyle 1-\dfrac{\lambda}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda)\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}-\dfrac{\left({\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}\right)^{2}}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{T}{N}-\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{1}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{T}{N}-\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{1+\dfrac{1}{T}\Tr\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]}+\delta_{1}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{T}{N}-\dfrac{T}{N}\frac{1}{1+\dfrac{p}{T}\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]}+\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}

(D.13)

with residual terms
\delta_{1}
and
\delta_{2}
. The detailed proof of
\max\left(\delta_{1},\delta_{2}\right)=o_{p}\left(T^{-{1}/{2}}\right)
can be found in the supplementary material of
Chen et al., (2011)
. Also, we know that

\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}m(-\lambda).

(D.14)

Thus based on (
D.13
) and (
D.14
), it is easy to get (
D.10
).

Limit of
F3
: Similar to the proof for
D3
, we examine the square of
F3
,
{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
, where the vectors on both the left and right sides of the quadratic form are identical. We have

\displaystyle{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}=

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\dfrac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right).

By adopting this approach, we can apply the same reasoning used in
F2
, where
\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}
is considered a vector of
N
random variables, each following a normal distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
. This allows us to invoke the trace Lemma (Lemma
1
), yielding:

\dfrac{T}{N}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left({\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\rightarrow 0,\quad\text{in probability}.

Subsequently,
\Tr\left({\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}
, is easily identifiable as exactly matching
F4
.

To sum up,

\left({\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\cdot\Phi_{2}(\lambda,\rho,H,G)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Given Assumption
D1
which assumes
T\rightarrow\infty
, it follows that
{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
converges to 0.

Limit of
F4
:

F4
is the same as the expression for bias of ridge regression in the paper
Hastie et al., (2022)
. Thus the proof can be found in its Supplementary document for proving for (A.12). What we have for
F4
is that

{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\Phi_{2}(\lambda,\rho,H,G).

(D.15)

Limit of
F5
: Similar to the proof in
F2
, we can rewrite
F5
as

\displaystyle{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}=

\displaystyle\left(\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right),

where
{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}=\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}/T
. By the same argument in
F2
, we have

\dfrac{T}{N}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\rightarrow 0,\quad\text{in probability}.

Also, we have

\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\left({\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}}+\lambda\bm{I}_{N}\right)^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\Theta_{2}(\lambda,\rho),

(D.16)

where
\Theta_{2}(\lambda,\rho)=\dfrac{1-\lambda m(-\lambda)}{\left[1-\rho+\rho\lambda m(-\lambda)\right]^{3}}-\lambda\dfrac{m(-\lambda)-\lambda m^{\prime}(-\lambda)}{\left[1-\rho+\rho\lambda m(-\lambda)\right]^{4}}
. Therefore,

{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\rho\Theta_{2}(\lambda,\rho).

The following is the detailed proof of (
D.16
), it employs the technique following
Ledoit and Péché, (2011)
based on the Stieltjes transform of the ESD of a random matrix. Multiplying
{\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda)
to both sides of the identity (
D.12
) (with
z=-\lambda
), taking trace, dividing by
N
, and using (
D.11
), we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]-\lambda\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[\left({\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda)\right)^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left[{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\dfrac{\left({\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}\right)\left({\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}\right)}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}{1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}}{\left(1+{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\tilde{\bm{y}}}_{t}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{(1/T)\Tr\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]}{\left(1+(1/T)\Tr\left[{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{R}}^{t}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]\right)^{2}}+\delta_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{(1/p)\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]}{\left(1+(N/T)(1/p)\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\right)^{2}}+\delta_{3}+\delta_{4}

(D.17)

with residuals
\delta_{3}
and
\delta_{4}
. Also we know that

\dfrac{d}{d\lambda}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\right]=-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[({\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda))^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}\right],

and observe that

\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[{\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda){\bm{\Sigma}}\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho).

From these premises, we define:

\delta_{5}=-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left[({\mathcal{R}}(-\lambda))^{2}{\bm{\Sigma}}\right]-\dfrac{\partial}{\partial\lambda}\Theta_{1}(\lambda,\rho).

To substantiate the convergence of
\max\left(\delta_{3},\delta_{4},\delta_{5}\right)=o_{p}\left(1\right)
, readers are referred to the supplementary material provided by
Chen et al., (2011)
. By reevaluating and rearranging Equation (
D.17
), we can deduce the identity specified in Equation (
D.16
).

Limit of
F6
: Mirroring the approach for
F3
,
F6
also presents an imbalance in its quadratic form. To address this, we analyze the square of
F6
, given by
{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\widehat{\bm{m}}}
. This formulation simplifies to:

\displaystyle\left({\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right)^{2}=

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\dfrac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{e}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sqrt{T}{\bar{{\bm{s}}}}\right).

Using the same rationale as in
F5
and invoking the Trace Lemma
1
, we deduce that

\dfrac{T}{N}\left({\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right)^{2}-\dfrac{1}{N}\Tr\left({\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\rightarrow 0,\quad\text{in probability}.

Furthermore,
\Tr\left({\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)={\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}
.

To sum up,

\left({\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\cdot{\bm{\mu}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle i.p.}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Given Assumption
D1
which assumes
T\rightarrow\infty
, it follows that
{\widehat{\bm{m}}}^{\top}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\Sigma}}{\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{\lambda}}^{-1}{\bm{\mu}}
converges to 0.
∎
